### 4.122 UI Design System Rollout — Scenarios, Financials, Lease Wizard (August 2026)
- **Context:** `TAILIQ_UI_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (locked 10 August 2026, Opus scoping session) re-skinned the app from the old dark-navy theme to a light "technical grey" paper theme. `styles.css` + body + `Dashboard.jsx` shipped as the initial re-skin unit; every other component file was left carrying its own old inline hex, to be swept file-by-file as each screen came up for review. This session did three of those sweeps, live-tested against real screenshots each round.
- **Root cause of every symptom below is the same:** components still had inline `style={{color:"#e2e8f0"}}` etc. — light-on-dark text/borders/pill fills chosen to read against the old dark-navy card background. Once `.card` flipped to `background: var(--color-soft-white)`, that same light-grey text became low-contrast-to-invisible against the new light background, and dark-navy panel fills (`#0d1e33`, `#0d1622`, etc.) sat inside a light card looking like a stray dark box.
- **`src/components/Scenarios.jsx`** (asset-level Scenarios tab):
  - All heading/label/value text swept from old dark-theme hex to `var(--color-carbon)`/`var(--color-graphite)`; shortfall reds/greens/ambers mapped to `--color-critical`/`--color-positive`/`--color-attention`.
  - The gold `#C9A84C` scenario accent (sliders, scenario chart line, "Explain" drawer) moved to teal, matching `styles.css`'s note that teal is now the app's one exploratory/action accent.
  - **Two-round fix on "Beyond horizon" / "Now within horizon" text:** first pass coloured these `var(--color-divider)` (correct per the design system's rule for literal "—" no-data dashes, wrongly extended to real informational text) — Alan flagged them as still too faint. Split the logic: genuine "—" placeholders stay divider-coloured, but "Beyond horizon"/"Now within horizon"/"No longer within horizon" (all meaningful state, not absence of data) now render in graphite.
  - **Three-round fix on the Base Case vs. Scenario chart line contrast:** round 1 (graphite vs. teal) and round 2 (graphite vs. carbon) both still read as "two dark grey lines" once the translucent area fills overlapped — pure value/hue differences weren't enough at this line weight. Final fix dual-encodes on three independent channels: Scenario gets its own hue (teal), a heavier 3px stroke (vs. Base Case's 2px graphite), and a dashed pattern — the distinction now holds even if any single channel is hard to read.
  - `MiniLineChart` (shared with `FlyForward.jsx`, imported by Scenarios) had its own hardcoded canvas-literal dark-theme colours — legend text, axis ticks, gridlines, and the lease-end divider/shading plugin. Chart.js draws straight to canvas and can't resolve CSS custom properties, so these stay literal hex, matched to graphite (`#687078`) and inner-divider (`#E8E7E2`) instead of the old navy/light-grey values.
- **`src/components/FlyForward.jsx`** (Financials tab — much larger sweep, same root cause across every sub-view): pot card headers (`FFPotCard` — the specific "feint pot headings" Alan flagged) and their status pills (anchored/at-risk/post-lease-shortfall) — pills moved from dark-tinted-bg-plus-bright-text to the design system's light-tint pattern (`var(--color-positive-tint)` + `var(--color-positive)` etc.); Assumptions panel; End of Lease Position (`EOLMoneyCard`/`EOLPhysicalCard` + wrapping card, gold border → teal, matching the "featured section" convention set in Scenarios); Forward Exposure Summary's three mini-stat panels; the top-level Fly-Forward description banner, Portfolio Shortfall Summary, missing-data/incomplete-calendar warning banners (→ attention amber), and Risk Peaks card; Record Completed Event modal + Pending Completions panel (→ attention amber, it's a genuine actionable-attention item) + Completed Events History; Maintenance Calendar's description banner, synthetic-pots info note (→ teal), data-completeness warning (→ attention), event-source legend swatches (derived/seasonality/airline-stated — remapped to graphite/teal/positive respectively so the three stay visually distinct as a *categorical* legend rather than colliding on one hue), and individual event rows; Seasonality Profile editor. The per-pot `MiniLineChart` dataset itself also had two canvas-literal near-white colours (`#e2e8f0` frozen-balance line and event-cost marker) that had gone from "crisp on dark" to "invisible on light" — fixed to graphite/carbon.
- **`src/components/LeaseWizard.jsx`** (Lease/Reserve Setup modal, all 4 steps): same sweep — modal title dropped its gold branding for carbon (consistent with other modal headers, e.g. Record Completed Event); tier-selection screen body/caption text → graphite; the "Details ▾" expandable-info links were blue (`#60a5fa`, explicitly disallowed by the design system's "no blue text anywhere" rule) → teal; Complete/In-progress/Outstanding pot-status pills → light-tint pattern; **the "+ Add Custom Pot" panel and other summary/extraction-result panels were on dark navy (`#0d1622`)**, which is what made the `btn-ghost` "+Add" button look like a solid dark fill in the screenshot (transparent-on-navy reads as filled; transparent-on-light reads correctly as a ghost button with a visible border) — panels moved to the neutral `var(--color-technical-grey)`, which fixed the button's apparent styling with no button-level change needed.
- **Validation:** all three files run through the standard two-step JSX check (`babel.transformSync` with `@babel/preset-react`, then `node --check` on the compiled output) after every edit round.
- **Not yet swept:** every other component still carries old dark-theme inline hex — asset details screen is next up per Alan (explicitly deferred this session, not forgotten), followed by whatever else surfaces on live-test.
- **Files touched:** `src/components/Scenarios.jsx`, `src/components/FlyForward.jsx`, `src/components/LeaseWizard.jsx`.
